Moemon Emerald Kai Card Guide

Where to find every card in the three card games: POKéMON cards, YU-GI-OH! and TRIPLE TRIAD. The special cards come first, the ones you won't get from ordinary card players, booster packs or the card shops. After them, every card of each game and every place it turns up. Version 1.57.1.

How cards come to youPOKéMON specialsYU-GI-OH! specialsTRIPLE TRIAD specialsAll POKéMON cardsAll YU-GI-OH! cardsAll TRIPLE TRIAD cards

How cards come to you

Register the CARD CASE and press its button in front of someone to ask for a game. These are the ordinary ways to collect cards. The special cards below are the ones they never give.

POKéMON cards

  • Booster packs. Every duel you win gives you a pack: ordinary duelists give one of their deck's kind, special trainers one of their own. Each pack has 10 cards, with a rare in every pack and a holo in about one pack in three. Packs are the only way to get holo cards (apart from trading with a friend).
  • A card from their deck. The first time each day you beat someone, you pick one card from their deck to keep (never a promo or a holo).
  • CARD CLUB counters (Oldale and Mauville POKéMON MARTS, the Lilycove DEPARTMENT STORE): trade in spare cards for CARD POINTS (CP) and buy any common or uncommon card with them.
  • GAME CENTER 2F CARD SHOP (Rustboro City): one copy of every non-holo card of the booster sets for money, the structure and theme decks, and the CARD CLUB counter's services.
  • Starter deck. Your first deck (Ember, Tidal or Garden Friends). Basic Energy is always free.

YU-GI-OH!

  • Booster packs from every win, and from the CARD CLUB for CP.
  • A card from their deck, the first time each day you beat someone.
  • The CARD CLUB sells every collectible card for CP, and the GAME CENTER 2F clerk sells one copy of each for money, plus the Starter and Structure Decks. So no YU-GI-OH! card is out of reach. The special duelists' signature cards are a free bonus for your first win over them.

TRIPLE TRIAD

  • Winning cards. When you win, you take cards from your opponent's hand. The town's trade rule decides how many: ONE (one card of your choice), DIFF (one per point you won by), DIRECT (the cards you turned) or ALL.
  • Sellers in seven POKéMON CENTERS and the GAME CENTER 2F: BRONZE (1★), SILVER (2★) and GOLD (3★) packs of 5 cards, and a few singles (1★ to 3★) that are different in each town. No one sells 4★ or 5★ cards.
  • 4★ cards are carried by ordinary players once you have 4 or more badges (more of them with 7 or more). 5★ cards are carried only by the special players below.

POKéMON cards: the special ones

44 of the 442 POKéMON cards are promo cards. They never come in packs, decks or shops. Each one is handed to you once, as a reward:

First win
A special trainer gives you their promo card the first time you beat them in a POKéMON card duel.
75% of a set
Own three quarters of a booster set's cards, then claim the promo at the counter's PRIZES tab (25% and 50% give packs and CP).
All GYM LEADERS
Beat all eight GYM LEADERS' decks, then claim it under PRIZES.
Card milestones
Duels won, packs opened, special trainers beaten, booster sets completed and more: claim each promo under PRIZES once you get there (what you did before counts too).

The special trainers

They play the full game with 6 Prize cards. Beating one also counts toward LEGENDS OF THE TABLE, and their deck becomes a recipe you can build in DECK RECIPES.

The Frontier Brains are only met inside Battle Frontier challenges.

Holo cards (95 of them) come only from booster packs: no shop sells them and no duelist lets you pick them. If you go a long time without a new holo from one kind of pack, the next rare from that kind is a holo you don't have yet.

YU-GI-OH!: the special ones

Every collectible YU-GI-OH! card (2307 of them) can be bought at the CARD CLUB with CARD POINTS. These cards are also a free bonus: each special duelist hands over their signature card the first time you beat them in a YU-GI-OH! duel. The four Elite Four members also give their POKéMON promo card along with it.

The CARD CLUB duelist (Oldale and Mauville POKéMON MARTS, Lilycove DEPARTMENT STORE) teaches the game and lends or gives a Starter Deck. Your first deck is a Starter Deck of your choice.

TRIPLE TRIAD: the special ones

No one sells 4★ or 5★ cards. All 69 5★ cards are carried by special players. Each of them brings one of their own 5★ cards each day, a different one from day to day. Beat them and take it with the town's trade rule (ONE lets you choose any card). The 83 4★ cards are carried by ordinary players once you have 4 or more badges, and by the special players. The full list is below.

With CARD SCALING on, opponents bring hands that suit yours. A special player keeps their 5★ card in the hand unless your deck is far too weak for it, so bring your best cards. The CARD QUEEN travels from town to town every few days and changes the local rules wherever she goes.

Every POKéMON card

Highlighted rows are cards you can't get from packs, shops or ordinary duelists. "Duelists" means ordinary card players with decks of that type (Fire: COOK, MAN, TEAM MAGMA GRUNT; Grass: TWIN, LITTLE GIRL, BUG CATCHER, PICNICKER, LASS, CAMPER; Lightning: GAME KID, MANIAC, SCIENTIST, DEVON WORKER, cycling TRIATHLETE; Water: TUBER, SWIMMER, SAILOR, FISHERMAN, TEAM AQUA GRUNT; Fighting: HIKER, BLACK BELT, running TRIATHLETE, EXPERT (man), MAN; Psychic: NINJA BOY, BEAUTY, PSYCHIC, HEX MANIAC, OLD WOMAN, EXPERT (woman); Colorless: BIG KID, YOUNGSTER, RICH BOY, MOéMON FAN, GENTLEMAN). "Special trainers" are the people whose decks you can pick the card from after a win.
